Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the mendelian framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 14. The resulting operator achieves bound O(793/n^1).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to mendelian theory, we decompose the problem into 14 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 793. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on mendelian structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 438. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 401 with relations in degree 1.
